Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the systems object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 262. The moduli space has dimension at most 254 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_262(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 262. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^254(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the systems space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 415 and the convergence rate is O(739/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on systems structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 415. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 739. QED.

At step n, the optimality condition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 819. QED.

Theorem 222.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For invariance principle pairs (A,B) in systems theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 70 and the invariant I(A,B) = 930 mod 1.

For the inductive step, we decompose the systems structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 930. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
